following Apache Camel's standard component architecture, derived from existing components like camel-timer and camel-kafka. The implementation provides:\n\n1. **Component Discovery**: The @Component(\"fix\") annotation enables Camel's service loader to automatically discover and instantiate the component\n2. **URI Endpoint Support**: The @UriEndpoint annotation with scheme=\"fix\" and syntax=\"fix:sessionID\" defines the URI format for consuming and producing FIX messages\n3. **Configuration Management**: FixConfiguration class with @UriParams and @UriParam annotations provides auto-wiring of URI parameters to configuration properties\n4. **Async Message Processing**: FixProducer extends DefaultAsyncProducer for non-blocking message sending\n5. **Consumer Lifecycle**: FixConsumer extends DefaultConsumer with proper doStart() and doStop() lifecycle methods\n\n### Design Decisions\n\n1. **Configuration Pattern**: Followed Kafka and AMQP components' pattern of using a separate @UriParams-annotated configuration class, enabling automatic parameter binding from URI query strings\n\n2. **Producer Pattern**: Used DefaultAsyncProducer to support asynchronous, non-blocking message production, suitable for network-based protocols like FIX\n\n3. **Consumer Pattern**: Implemented basic DefaultConsumer with lifecycle management hooks (doStart/doStop) to manage FIX acceptor sessions\n\n4. **Constants**: Defined header constants for FIX message metadata (message type, session ID, sender/target company IDs) following Camel's convention\n\n5. **Minimal Dependencies**: Only depends on camel-support, keeping the component lightweight. A full FIX implementation would add a FIX protocol library like quickfixj\n\n### Integration with Build System\n\n- Created `pom.xml` with parent reference to `components` module (inherited from camel-parent v4.18.0)\n- Registered the new module in `components/pom.xml` in alphabetical order (between camel-fhir and camel-file-watch)\n- Module inherits standard build properties from parent, including test configuration and plugin management\n\n### Future Implementation Steps\n\nTo make this component production-ready:\n\n1. **Add FIX Protocol Library**: Add dependency on QuickFIX/J or similar FIX protocol library\n2. **Implement FIX Engine Integration**: Wire the FIX protocol engine to send/receive messages through the Camel routes\n3. **Session Management**: Implement FIX session lifecycle management (connect, disconnect, heartbeat)\n4. **Error Handling**: Add comprehensive error handling for FIX protocol-specific errors\n5. **Message Conversion**: Implement conversion between FIX protocol messages and Camel Exchange format\n6. **Unit Tests**: Add comprehensive test coverage for component lifecycle and message processing\n7. **Documentation**: Add Asciidoc documentation (fix-component.adoc) explaining usage and options\n\n### Compliance with Camel Standards\n\nThe implementation adheres to Apache Camel component architecture standards:\n- ✅ Component extends DefaultComponent\n- ✅ Endpoint extends DefaultEndpoint with @UriEndpoint annotation\n- ✅ Producer extends DefaultAsyncProducer with async process() method\n- ✅ Consumer extends DefaultConsumer with lifecycle management\n- ✅ Configuration uses @UriParams and @UriParam annotations\n- ✅ Proper logging with SLF4J\n- ✅ Apache License headers on all files\n- ✅ Module registration in parent pom.xml\n- ✅ Follows naming conventions and patterns from existing following Apache Camel's standard component architecture, derived from existing components like camel-timer and camel-kafka. The implementation provides:\n\n1. **Component Discovery**: The @Component(\"fix\") annotation enables Camel's service loader to automatically discover and instantiate the component\n2. **URI Endpoint Support**: The @UriEndpoint annotation with scheme=\"fix\" and syntax=\"fix:sessionID\" defines the URI format for consuming and producing FIX messages\n3. **Configuration Management**: FixConfiguration class with @UriParams and @UriParam annotations provides auto-wiring of URI parameters to configuration properties\n4. **Async Message Processing**: FixProducer extends DefaultAsyncProducer for non-blocking message sending\n5. **Consumer Lifecycle**: FixConsumer extends DefaultConsumer with proper doStart() and doStop() lifecycle methods\n\n### Design Decisions\n\n1. **Configuration Pattern**: Followed Kafka and AMQP components' pattern of using a separate @UriParams-annotated configuration class, enabling automatic parameter binding from URI query strings\n\n2. **Producer Pattern**: Used DefaultAsyncProducer to support asynchronous, non-blocking message production, suitable for network-based protocols like FIX\n\n3. **Consumer Pattern**: Implemented basic DefaultConsumer with lifecycle management hooks (doStart/doStop) to manage FIX acceptor sessions\n\n4. **Constants**: Defined header constants for FIX message metadata (message type, session ID, sender/target company IDs) following Camel's convention\n\n5. **Minimal Dependencies**: Only depends on camel-support, keeping the component lightweight.
